           Spinach Caramelized Onion And Muenster Au
                                      Gratin Potatoes
Ingredients                        Directions
8 red potatoes, cubed             Preheat an oven to 400 degrees F (200 degrees C). Combine the
5 ounces fresh spinach leaves     potatoes and spinach in a 9x13-inch baking dish; set aside.
1/4 cup butter
1 onion, sliced                   Melt 3 tablespoons of butter in a saucepan over medium-low heat.
1 teaspoon minced garlic          Stir in the onion, and cook until dark brown, about 15 minutes. Stir
3 tablespoons all-purpose flour   in the garlic and flour; cook 1 minute more. Season with salt, and
1/2 teaspoon salt                 stir in the milk and cream. Bring to a simmer over medium heat;
                                  cook and stir until the sauce has thickened, about 10 minutes. Stir
1 cup milk
                                  in 3/4 of the Muenster cheese until melted, and pour over the
1 cup heavy cream
                                  potatoes.
2 cups shredded Muenster
cheese                            Cover the potatoes with aluminum foil, and bake in the preheated
                                  oven 30 minutes. Uncover, and cook 20 minutes more, then sprinkle
                                  with the remaining Muenster cheese, return to the oven, and bake
                                  until the potatoes are tender and the cheese is bubbly, about 10
                                  minutes more.

                                           Warm Chicken Spinach Salad
Ingredients                         Directions
3 cups torn fresh spinach           On two salad plates, arrange spinach, oranges, mushrooms and
1 (11 ounce) can mandarin           onion; set aside. In a skillet, saute chicken and walnuts in oil until
oranges, drained                    chicken is no longer pink. In a small bowl, combine the cornstarch,
1/2 cup sliced fresh mushrooms      ginger, orange juice and vinegar until smooth; stir into the chicken
3 slices red onion, halved          mixture. Bring to a boil; cook and stir for 2 minutes or until
1/2 pound skinless, boneless        thickened and bubbly. Pour over salads and serving immediately.
chicken breast halves - cut into
strips
1/4 cup chopped walnuts
2 tablespoons olive or vegetable
oil
2 teaspoons cornstarch
1/2 teaspoon ground ginger
1/4 cup orange juice
1/4 cup red wine vinegar or cider

1 cup butter                           Preheat an oven to 400 degrees F (200 degrees C). Line baking

phyllo dough, thawed
                                       Place a phyllo sheet onto a flat surface, and brush it lightly with
                                       melted butter. Place a second sheet on top of the first sheet, and
                                       brush with melted butter. Cut each stack of 2 buttered sheets into 5
                                       strips about 3 1/2 inches wide by 13 inches long. Cover strips
                                       waiting to be filled with a damp cloth while you fill and roll the
                                       triangles.

                                       To fold a triangle, place a tablespoon of filling centered at the top
                                       end of a phyllo dough strip, and fold the right corner across and
                                       down, to make a pointed, filled dough pocket at the top of the strip.
                                       Fold the pocket down one more turn, like folding a flag, to make a
                                       triangle shape. Continue to fold the triangle down and across the
                                       dough strip until you reach the end of the strip and you have a
                                       compact, filled triangle of dough. Repeat with the remaining filling
                                       and phyllo dough.

                                       Place the filled triangles onto the prepared baking sheets, and brush
                                       them with butter. Working in batches if necessary, bake in the
                                       preheated oven for 12 to 15 minutes per batch, until the triangles
                                       are golden brown.
